G* for mysql (generated by GTrans from G and T)
===============================================

Notation. X[ℓ] is the labelled non-terminal X_ℓ of the note. A rule reads
    X[ℓ] → u  ⇒  u′      where x1: Y1[ℓ1], …     ℓ = r ∘ (σ′1⊗ℓ1) ∘ … = ℓ
u is what the rule adds to the gold query Q, u′ what it adds to the mutant query Q′.
A copy rule has u = u′ and shows u once. Shared children x1, x2, … appear on both sides; a fresh
subtree z is sampled from G and appears on one side only. k and k′ are constants of the column.
Start symbols: Stmt[↑], Stmt[↓], Stmt[=]   (metRel(Q, Q′) = ⊆, ⊇, = respectively).

Rules after reduction: 4294   (before reduction: 6069)
By kind: copy 688, swap 674, insert 69, drop 69, extreme 12, constant 4, law 2778

        where x: H[↑]
        ℓ = (=) ∘ (+⊗↑) = ↑        [law L3 (involution)]
    H[↑] → x  ⇒  NOT ((NOT (x)))
        where x: H[↑]
        ℓ = (=) ∘ (+⊗↑) = ↑        [law L3 (involution)]
    H[↑] → NOT (((x) AND (y)))  ⇒  ((NOT (x))) OR ((NOT (y)))
        where x: H[id], y: H[↓]
        ℓ = (=) ∘ (−⊗id) ∘ (−⊗↓) = ↑        [law L4 (De Morgan)]
    H[↑] → NOT (((x) AND (y)))  ⇒  ((NOT (x))) OR ((NOT (y)))

    H[↓] → z  ⇒  FALSE

        ℓ = ↓ = ↓        [extreme: FALSE_H is the least H]

  [swap]  273 rules
    Q[↓] → SELECT a.c0, a.c1, a.c2 FROM x1 AS a WHERE x2  ⇒  SELECT DISTINCT a.c0, a.c1, a.c2 FROM x1 AS a WHERE x2
        where x1: Tbl[id], x2: CA[id]
        ℓ = ↓ ∘ (0⊗id) ∘ (+⊗id) = ↓        [order: SELD ≼ SEL]
    Q[↓] → SELECT a.c0, a.c1, a.c2 FROM x1 AS a WHERE x2  ⇒  SELECT DISTINCT a.c0, a.c1, a.c2 FROM x1 AS a WHERE x2
        where x1: Tbl[id], x2: CA[=]
        ℓ = ↓ ∘ (0⊗id) ∘ (+⊗=) = ↓        [order: SELD ≼ SEL]
    Q[↓] → SELECT a.c0, a.c1, a.c2 FROM x1 AS a WHERE x2  ⇒  SELECT DISTINCT a.c0, a.c1, a.c2 FROM x1 AS a WHERE x2
        where x1: Tbl[id], x2: CA[↓]
        ℓ = ↓ ∘ (0⊗id) ∘ (+⊗↓) = ↓        [order: SELD ≼ SEL]

        ℓ = (+⊗=) = =        [copy AGG_MAX]
    V[=] → SUM(a.c0)
        where x1: Q[=]
        ℓ = (0⊗=) = =        [copy AGG_SUM]
    V[=] → COUNT(a.c0)
        where x1: Q[=]
        ℓ = (+⊗=) = =        [copy AGG_CNT]
    V[=] → COUNT(DISTINCT a.c0)
        where x1: Q[=]
        ℓ = (+⊗=) = =        [copy AGG_CNTD]
    V[=] → COUNT(*)
        where x1: Q[=]
        ℓ = (+⊗=) = =        [copy AGG_CNTS]
    V[=] → x1 + x2
        where x1: V[=], x2: KPos[id]
        ℓ = (+⊗=) ∘ (+⊗id) = =        [copy PLUS]

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
V[id]   (8 rules)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  [copy]  8 rules
    V[id] → MIN(a.c0)
        where x1: Q[id]
        ℓ = (0⊗id) = id        [copy AGG_MIN]

        where x1: Q[id]
        ℓ = (0⊗id) = id        [copy AGG_AVG]

        where x1: Q[id]
        ℓ = (+⊗id) = id        [copy AGG_MAX]
    V[id] → SUM(a.c0)
        where x1: Q[id]
        ℓ = (0⊗id) = id        [copy AGG_SUM]
    V[id] → COUNT(a.c0)
        where x1: Q[id]
        ℓ = (+⊗id) = id        [copy AGG_CNT]
    V[id] → COUNT(DISTINCT a.c0)
        where x1: Q[id]
        ℓ = (+⊗id) = id        [copy AGG_CNTD]
    V[id] → COUNT(*)
        where x1: Q[id]
        ℓ = (+⊗id) = id        [copy AGG_CNTS]
    V[id] → x1 + x2
        where x1: V[id], x2: KPos[id]
        ℓ = (+⊗id) ∘ (+⊗id) = id        [copy PLUS]
